# 备份恢复认证：接口与业务规格\n2: \n3: 修复缺失实现，依据真实输入生成可重建、确定性的JSON产物。以下业务算法的节点、边、窗口或资源是被实现程序的数据语义，不是Agent执行计划。\n4: \n5: ## 实现范围与保护边界\n6: \n7: 组件目录为`products/backup_graph/`、`products/journal_restore/`、`products/backup_windows/`。每个组件实现model.py、transform.py、render.py；根目录实现run_all.py、final_report.py。可添加必要的私有辅助模块。\n8: 不得修改README.md、任务说明、inputs/、任何contract.json、check_module.py、根check.py或integrate.py。已有受保护稳定接口保留。\n9: \n10: 本题初始工作区提供受保护的materialize.py。使用`python3 materialize.py`生成输入、产品契约、局部检查及缺失实现占位；这是材料准备操作，不生成业务答案。脚本校验嵌入清单SHA-256；重复执行保留已有实现并检查受保护生成文件，冲突必须失败。不得改物化器或生成的输入/契约/检查。\n11: \n12: ## 公共函数接口与输入解释\n13: \n14: - `model.load_payload(path: Path)`：读取UTF-8 JSON，对象或数组顶层合法；非法JSON、顶层null/布尔/数字/字符串均抛ValueError。业务字段由transform检查。\n15: - `transform.transform(payloads: list, contract: dict)`：payloads严格按该契约inputs的顺序传入已读取的对象或数组，返回`{\"name\": contract[\"name\"], \"result\": 算法结果对象}`。输出恰有name/result两层；本说明各operation的输出字段均属于result，不得额外嵌套一次name/result。不得修改payloads或contract。\n16: - 输入对象仅当键集合恰好是`{\"name\",\"result\"}`时视为上游组件封装；先读取其result，再使用对应operation约定的字段。原始本地对象不是文件路径，也不当成records或nodes列表。多输入operation必须消费全部指定输入，不能复制固定上游结果。\n17: - `render.build_component(root: Path, contract_path: Path)`：读取该契约及其全部输入，调用transform并返回完整封装；执行组件render.py时写入contract.output。文件路径相对于工作区root；从任意当前目录启动均应正确，缺文件或子命令失败须失败。\n18: - int字段严格排除bool；有限数字允许int/float但排除bool、NaN、Infinity。非空文本先检查去首尾空白后不为空；是否返回去空白/小写形式由各operation明确规定，不全局改写身份字段。列表与字典按规定类型读取，不把null当空集合，不自动把数字字符串转换成数字。\n19: - 文中没有给出默认值的必要字段缺失、非法类型或明确列出的冲突抛ValueError；合法无解使用operation的无解结果，不等同于输入错误。词典序采用通常字符串/列表/元组顺序，相同前缀时较短列表更小。\n20: - contract中的name/operation/inputs/output是接口字段；只有下列业务规则显式使用的算法参数参与计算，不能看到threshold便对所有operation筛选。参数值读取本组件契约，不从样例推断固定常量。\n21: \n22: 本题强化operation（recovery_chain, restore_join, transaction_replay）还要求整个payloads中的嵌套JSON值有限，即使字段未参与业务计算，内含NaN/Infinity也抛ValueError。\n23: \n24: ## 各组件的完整业务规则\n25: \n26: ### products/backup_graph — 分叉备份链的恢复点与成本优化\n27: \n28: operation：`recovery_chain`。具体输入文件、输入顺序和输出路径读取本目录contract.json；下文只定义文件内容的解释，不另给任务调度关系。\n29: \n30: 输入 {target:int>=0,snapshots:[{id,kind:full/incremental,parent:str或null,start:int>=0,end:int>start,cost:int>=0,epoch:int>=0,valid:bool,state:[{key,value:JSON值}]}]}。snapshot ID与每份state的key唯一，未知parent或任意parent环ValueError。full必须parent=null且start=0；incremental只有parent有效、parent.end==start且epoch相等才可恢复（不满足为不可恢复，不是schema错误）。valid=false的节点及其后代不可恢复；只有end<=target可选。先最大化恢复点end，再最小化整条链cost，再按路径ID列表字典序最小选一条。输出 {feasible,chain:list[id],snapshot_end:int,cost:int,state:按key排序的完整恢复点状态}；无可恢复快照时false/[]/0/0/[]。\n31: \n32: 补充边界：state表示该快照终点的完整恢复点状态，不是增量patch；最终state直接取所选链末快照的state，不把祖先state再合并进来。链成本是所选路径各快照cost之和。需要检查所有parent分量中的环和未知引用，即使快照invalid或end超过target也不能隐藏schema错误。incremental的parent=null是不可恢复，非full的schema失败；full的非null parent或start!=0才是schema错误。无可恢复快照与可行的空state快照有不同feasible值。\n33: \n34: 局部检查命令：`python3 products/backup_graph/check_module.py`。运行检查时，契约引用的输入与本组件输出必须真实存在；缺少条件时先完成实际所需的工作，不能吞掉错误或跳过检查。\n35: \n36: ### products/journal_restore — 连续LSN与原子事务重放\n37: \n38: operation：`transaction_replay`。具体输入文件、输入顺序和输出路径读取本目录contract.json；下文只定义文件内容的解释，不另给任务调度关系。\n39: \n40: 输入 {checkpoint:int>=-1,records:[{lsn:int>=0,epoch:int>=0,tx:str,kind:write/commit/abort,...}]}。write含key:str、value:JSON值、seq:int>=0；commit含count:int>=0；abort无额外必需字段。每lsn取最高epoch，任意相同lsn/epoch内容冲突ValueError，相同内容去重；从checkpoint+1连续扫描，首缺口停止。按LSN积累每tx的writes；commit须seq恰为0..count-1，每key可重复并按seq覆盖，之后才一次输出原子事务；缺写commit标为incomplete，不输出部分写。abort丢弃事务。已扫描前缀内commit/abort后又出现同tx记录ValueError。输出 {watermark:int,transactions:[{tx,commit_lsn,writes:[{key,value}],duration:int}],incomplete:list[tx],incomplete_commits:[{tx,commit_lsn}]}；transactions和incomplete_commits按(commit_lsn,tx)，duration为write条数，writes按seq，incomplete含未结束/缺写事务并按tx排序。空records返回checkpoint及空集合。\n41: \n42: 补充边界：同LSN/epoch冲突检查覆盖全部版本，包括被较高epoch覆盖或位于扫描缺口之后者。事务执行只针对checkpoint后的连续LSN前缀，watermark是实际扫描到的最后LSN，包括abort和不完整commit；不是最后成功commit。已成功commit的writes保留全部按seq排序的写入，包括重复key，不提前压缩成键值表；后续restore应用时才由后写覆盖。seq重复、缺失或越界都属于不完整commit，写入不部分提交。count=0且无写时可以提交零duration事务。缺口后记录不影响pending/ended执行状态，但仍需完成字段与版本冲突校验。\n43: \n44: 局部检查命令：`python3 products/journal_restore/check_module.py`。运行检查时，契约引用的输入与本组件输出必须真实存在；缺少条件时先完成实际所需的工作，不能吞掉错误或跳过检查。\n45: \n46: ### products/backup_windows — 快照与原子日志的恢复一致性核验\n47: \n48: operation：`restore_join`。具体输入文件、输入顺序和输出路径读取本目录contract.json；下文只定义文件内容的解释，不另给任务调度关系。\n49: \n50: 输入顺序为 {target:int>=0,rto:int>=0,expected_sha256:64位小写hex}、recovery_chain结果、transaction_replay结果。要求snapshot可行、snapshot_end<=target、日志watermark>=target，且snapshot_end<commit_lsn<=target范围没有incomplete_commits。取该范围的原子事务按commit顺序应用到snapshot.state；不能应用仅有部分writes或aborted事务。耗时=备份链cost+所应用事务duration之和。输出 {feasible,reason:ok/snapshot/gap/transaction/rto/digest,applied:list[tx],state:[{key,value}],sha256:str,cost:int}，state按key；digest为state规范紧凑JSON的SHA256。snapshot/gap/transaction失败输出空applied/state、sha256为空、cost=0；其余失败保留计算结果。失败优先级为snapshot、gap、transaction、rto、digest，不能因目标摘要恰好一致而忽略缺写的已提交事务。\n51: \n52: 补充边界：base.state为完整起点，事务范围严格是(snapshot_end,target]。按(commit_lsn,tx)应用每个原子事务，内部按其writes给定seq顺序覆盖key，保留未被覆盖的快照键；不能只保留日志键或把snapshot_end这一刻的commit再应用。rto/digest失败时也返回已经计算的applied/state/sha256/cost；snapshot/gap/transaction失败才清空这些字段。无应用事务时仍以快照成本和状态计算摘要并检查rto/digest。\n53: \n54: 局部检查命令：`python3 products/backup_windows/check_module.py`。运行检查时，契约引用的输入与本组件输出必须真实存在；缺少条件时先完成实际所需的工作，不能吞掉错误或跳过检查。\n55: \n56: ## JSON产物、集成与最终报告\n57: \n58: 所有写出的JSON文件采用UTF-8、ensure_ascii=False、sort_keys=True、indent=2、allow_nan=False，末尾加一个真实换行字符。紧凑摘要序列化与文件展示格式不同。\n59: `run_all.py`定位工作区，依据真实文件接口完成全部必要计算，执行已有集成与最终报告。不能用文件名排序代替执行条件，不能依赖旧outputs；各子进程失败传播非零退出码。已有`python3 integrate.py`读取组件产物生成`outputs/core_manifest.json`，其完整结构为`{\"components\": {组件名: 该组件的result}}`。\n60: \n61: `final_report.py`读取并解析完整`outputs/core_manifest.json`对象，写`outputs/final_report.json`，恰有title、component_count、sha256、attention四个字段。title准确为`备份恢复认证`；component_count为core_object[\"components\"]的键数。\n62: sha256的输入对象是完整core_object，包含外层components键；不是仅core_object[\"components\"]、不是原文件字节、不是多个文件摘要拼接。序列化准确为`json.dumps(core_object, ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True, separators=(\",\", \":\"), allow_nan=False).encode(\"utf-8\")`，不加文件末尾换行，然后求SHA-256小写hex。\n63: attention是组件名去重排序列表：对每个result，blocked为真、feasible或compatible显式为false、deny>0、score_total>=20中任一条件成立即列入；不存在的字段不触发，不能把空列表、空对象或数值0等同于显式false。每个组件至多列一次。\n64: \n65: ## 验收与执行边界\n66: \n67: 在工作区运行`python3 check.py`。公开检查会清理旧outputs、完成适用的材料物化、运行run_all并检查模块与输出结构；公开通过不代表全部业务语义正确。外部只读验证器另外检查受保护文件、全部预期产物、规范序列化及接口边界输入。\n68: 给Agent的材料只有本任务TASK.md和workspace。作者参考实现、评分器、审计记录、历史输出和其他任务不得作为工作区或模型上下文输入。\n\n(End of 备份恢复认证：接口与业务规格\n\n修复缺失实现，依据真实输入生成可重建、确定性的JSON产物。以下业务算法的节点、边、窗口或资源是被实现程序的数据语义，不是Agent执行计划。\n\n## 实现范围与保护边界\n\n组件目录为`products/backup_graph/`、`products/journal_restore/`、`products/backup_windows/`。每个组件实现model.py、transform.py、render.py；根目录实现run_all.py、final_report.py。可添加必要的私有辅助模块。\n不得修改README.md、任务说明、inputs/、任何contract.json、check_module.py、根check.py或integrate.py。已有受保护稳定接口保留。\n\n本题初始工作区提供受保护的materialize.py。使用`python3 materialize.py`生成输入、产品契约、局部检查及缺失实现占位；这是材料准备操作，不生成业务答案。脚本校验嵌入清单SHA-256；重复执行保留已有实现并检查受保护生成文件，冲突必须失败。不得改物化器或生成的输入/契约/检查。\n\n## 公共函数接口与输入解释\n\n- `model.load_payload(path: Path)`：读取UTF-8 JSON，对象或数组顶层合法；非法JSON、顶层null/布尔/数字/字符串均抛ValueError。业务字段由transform检查。\n- `transform.transform(payloads: list, contract: dict)`：payloads严格按该契约inputs的顺序传入已读取的对象或数组，返回`{\"name\": contract[\"name\"], \"result\": 算法结果对象}`。输出恰有name/result两层；本说明各operation的输出字段均属于result，不得额外嵌套一次name/result。不得修改payloads或contract。\n- 输入对象仅当键集合恰好是`{\"name\",\"result\"}`时视为上游组件封装；先读取其result，再使用对应operation约定的字段。原始本地对象不是文件路径，也不当成records或nodes列表。多输入operation必须消费全部指定输入，不能复制固定上游结果。\n- `render.build_component(root: Path, contract_path: Path)`：读取该契约及其全部输入，调用transform并返回完整封装；执行组件render.py时写入contract.output。文件路径相对于工作区root；从任意当前目录启动均应正确，缺文件或子命令失败须失败。\n- int字段严格排除bool；有限数字允许int/float但排除bool、NaN、Infinity。非空文本先检查去首尾空白后不为空；是否返回去空白/小写形式由各operation明确规定，不全局改写身份字段。列表与字典按规定类型读取，不把null当空集合，不自动把数字字符串转换成数字。\n- 文中没有给出默认值的必要字段缺失、非法类型或明确列出的冲突抛ValueError；合法无解使用operation的无解结果，不等同于输入错误。词典序采用通常字符串/列表/元组顺序，相同前缀时较短列表更小。\n- contract中的name/operation/inputs/output是接口字段；只有下列业务规则显式使用的算法参数参与计算，不能看到threshold便对所有operation筛选。参数值读取本组件契约，不从样例推断固定常量。\n\n本题强化operation（recovery_chain, restore_join, transaction_replay）还要求整个payloads中的嵌套JSON值有限，即使字段未参与业务计算，内含NaN/Infinity也抛ValueError。\n\n## 各组件的完整业务规则\n\n### products/backup_graph — 分叉备份链的恢复点与成本优化\n\noperation：`recovery_chain`。具体输入文件、输入顺序和输出路径读取本目录contract.json；下文只定义文件内容的解释，不另给任务调度关系。\n\n输入 {target:int>=0,snapshots:[{id,kind:full/incremental,parent:str或null,start:int>=0,end:int>start,cost:int>=0,epoch:int>=0,valid:bool,state:[{key,value:JSON值}]}]}。snapshot ID与每份state的key唯一，未知parent或任意parent环ValueError。full必须parent=null且start=0；incremental只有parent有效、parent.end==start且epoch相等才可恢复（不满足为不可恢复，不是schema错误）。valid=false的节点及其后代不可恢复；只有end<=target可选。先最大化恢复点end，再最小化整条链cost，再按路径ID列表字典序最小选一条。输出 {feasible,chain:list[id],snapshot_end:int,cost:int,state:按key排序的完整恢复点状态}；无可恢复快照时false/[]/0/0/[]。\n\n补充边界：state表示该快照终点的完整恢复点状态，不是增量patch；最终state直接取所选链末快照的state，不把祖先state再合并进来。链成本是所选路径各快照cost之和。需要检查所有parent分量中的环和未知引用，即使快照invalid或end超过target也不能隐藏schema错误。incremental的parent=null是不可恢复，非full的schema失败；full的非null parent或start!=0才是schema错误。无可恢复快照与可行的空state快照有不同feasible值。\n\n局部检查命令：`python3 products/backup_graph/check_module.py`。运行检查时，契约引用的输入与本组件输出必须真实存在；缺少条件时先完成实际所需的工作，不能吞掉错误或跳过检查。\n\n### products/journal_restore — 连续LSN与原子事务重放\n\noperation：`transaction_replay`。具体输入文件、输入顺序和输出路径读取本目录contract.json；下文只定义文件内容的解释，不另给任务调度关系。\n\n输入 {checkpoint:int>=-1,records:[{lsn:int>=0,epoch:int>=0,tx:str,kind:write/commit/abort,...}]}。write含key:str、value:JSON值、seq:int>=0；commit含count:int>=0；abort无额外必需字段。每lsn取最高epoch，任意相同lsn/epoch内容冲突ValueError，相同内容去重；从checkpoint+1连续扫描，首缺口停止。按LSN积累每tx的writes；commit须seq恰为0..count-1，每key可重复并按seq覆盖，之后才一次输出原子事务；缺写commit标为incomplete，不输出部分写。abort丢弃事务。已扫描前缀内commit/abort后又出现同tx记录ValueError。输出 {watermark:int,transactions:[{tx,commit_lsn,writes:[{key,value}],duration:int}],incomplete:list[tx],incomplete_commits:[{tx,commit_lsn}]}；transactions和incomplete_commits按(commit_lsn,tx)，duration为write条数，writes按seq，incomplete含未结束/缺写事务并按tx排序。空records返回checkpoint及空集合。\n\n补充边界：同LSN/epoch冲突检查覆盖全部版本，包括被较高epoch覆盖或位于扫描缺口之后者。事务执行只针对checkpoint后的连续LSN前缀，watermark是实际扫描到的最后LSN，包括abort和不完整commit；不是最后成功commit。已成功commit的writes保留全部按seq排序的写入，包括重复key，不提前压缩成键值表；后续restore应用时才由后写覆盖。seq重复、缺失或越界都属于不完整commit，写入不部分提交。count=0且无写时可以提交零duration事务。缺口后记录不影响pending/ended执行状态，但仍需完成字段与版本冲突校验。\n\n局部检查命令：`python3 products/journal_restore/check_module.py`。运行检查时，契约引用的输入与本组件输出必须真实存在；缺少条件时先完成实际所需的工作，不能吞掉错误或跳过检查。\n\n### products/backup_windows — 快照与原子日志的恢复一致性核验\n\noperation：`restore_join`。具体输入文件、输入顺序和输出路径读取本目录contract.json；下文只定义文件内容的解释，不另给任务调度关系。\n\n输入顺序为 {target:int>=0,rto:int>=0,expected_sha256:64位小写hex}、recovery_chain结果、transaction_replay结果。要求snapshot可行、snapshot_end<=target、日志watermark>=target，且snapshot_end<commit_lsn<=target范围没有incomplete_commits。取该范围的原子事务按commit顺序应用到snapshot.state；不能应用仅有部分writes或aborted事务。耗时=备份链cost+所应用事务duration之和。输出 {feasible,reason:ok/snapshot/gap/transaction/rto/digest,applied:list[tx],state:[{key,value}],sha256:str,cost:int}，state按key；digest为state规范紧凑JSON的SHA256。snapshot/gap/transaction失败输出空applied/state、sha256为空、cost=0；其余失败保留计算结果。失败优先级为snapshot、gap、transaction、rto、digest，不能因目标摘要恰好一致而忽略缺写的已提交事务。\n\n补充边界：base.state为完整起点，事务范围严格是(snapshot_end,target]。按(commit_lsn,tx)应用每个原子事务，内部按其writes给定seq顺序覆盖key，保留未被覆盖的快照键；不能只保留日志键或把snapshot_end这一刻的commit再应用。rto/digest失败时也返回已经计算的applied/state/sha256/cost；snapshot/gap/transaction失败才清空这些字段。无应用事务时仍以快照成本和状态计算摘要并检查rto/digest。\n\n局部检查命令：`python3 products/backup_windows/check_module.py`。运行检查时，契约引用的输入与本组件输出必须真实存在；缺少条件时先完成实际所需的工作，不能吞掉错误或跳过检查。\n\n## JSON产物、集成与最终报告\n\n所有写出的JSON文件采用UTF-8、ensure_ascii=False、sort_keys=True、indent=2、allow_nan=False，末尾加一个真实换行字符。紧凑摘要序列化与文件展示格式不同。\n`run_all.py`定位工作区，依据真实文件接口完成全部必要计算，执行已有集成与最终报告。不能用文件名排序代替执行条件，不能依赖旧outputs；各子进程失败传播非零退出码。已有`python3 integrate.py`读取组件产物生成`outputs/core_manifest.json`，其完整结构为`{\"components\": {组件名: 该组件的result}}`。\n\n`final_report.py`读取并解析完整`outputs/core_manifest.json`对象，写`outputs/final_report.json`，恰有title、component_count、sha256、attention四个字段。title准确为`备份恢复认证`；component_count为core_object[\"components\"]的键数。\nsha256的输入对象是完整core_object，包含外层components键；不是仅core_object[\"components\"]、不是原文件字节、不是多个文件摘要拼接。序列化准确为`json.dumps(core_object, ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True, separators=(\",\", \":\"), allow_nan=False).encode(\"utf-8\")`，不加文件末尾换行，然后求SHA-256小写hex。\nattention是组件名去重排序列表：对每个result，blocked为真、feasible或compatible显式为false、deny>0、score_total>=20中任一条件成立即列入；不存在的字段不触发，不能把空列表、空对象或数值0等同于显式false。每个组件至多列一次。\n\n## Each snapshot's state is the complete recovery point state at that snapshot's end. So when we select a chain ending at some snapshot, the final state is that snapshot's state directly (sorted by key). No merging.\n\nWait, but then for \"base\" snapshot, state is `[{key:k, value:1}]`. For \"inc\" snapshot, state is `[{key:b,value:5},{key:k,value:4}]`. So final state for chain [base, inc] would be inc's state, sorted by key = [{b:5},{k:4}].\n\nHmm, but what does \"recoverable\" mean for the chain? Let me think about the chain validity:\n- A full snapshot with parent=null, start=0 is recoverable (if valid and end<=target)\n- An incremental snapshot is recoverable if parent valid AND parent.end==start AND epoch equal (and valid, and end<=target)\n- valid=false nodes and descendants not recoverable\n\nSo the recoverable set is a set of snapshots (nodes). The chain is a path from root (full) down through increments. Since full must parent=null and start=0, and incremental parent must be valid (recoverable), and parent.end==start, epoch equal.\n\nActually, the chain: we want to select a chain from some full root to some end snapshot. Each incremental's parent must be recoverable (parent chain exists). The chain cost = sum of costs along path.\n\nWait, but there's ambiguity: is a \"recoverable snapshot\" a snapshot that can be recovered by following its parent chain all the way to a full root? Yes. Let me define:\n\nA snapshot S is recoverable if:\n- S is valid\n- S.end <= target\n- If full: parent==null and start==0 (already enforced as schema for full) — but full is inherently recoverable if valid & end<=target\n- If incremental: parent is not null, parent is a known snapshot, parent is valid, parent.end == S.start, parent.epoch == S.epoch, and parent is itself recoverable (transitively).\n\nActually \"parent valid\" — let me re-read: \"incremental只有parent有效、parent.end==start且epoch相等才可恢复\". parent有效 means parent snapshot valid. Also \"valid=false的节点及其后代不可恢复\". So parent must be valid AND recoverable.\n\nSo recoverability is transitive from a valid full snapshot.\n\nNow the chain selection: maximize end (recovery point), then minimize cost, then lexicographically smallest path ID list.\n\nLet me think about what \"chain\" is. The chain is a list of snapshot IDs from root full to the selected end snapshot. The path ID list lexicographic comparison.\n\nBut wait, could there be a case where the chain is just a single full snapshot? Yes, if full is the best.\n\nLet me also think: does \"maximize recovery point end\" mean the max end among recoverable snapshots? same content dedupe)\n- From checkpoint+1 continuous scan, stop at first gap\n- Accumulate per-tx writes by LSN\n- commit: seq exactly 0..count-1; each key can repeat, overwrite by seq; after that output atomic transaction once\n- missing-write commit → incomplete, don't output partial writes\n- abort discards transaction\n- within scanned prefix, after commit/abort, same tx record again → ValueError\n- output {watermark, transactions:[{tx,commit_lsn,writes:[{key,value}],duration}], incomplete:list[tx], incomplete_commits:[{tx,commit_lsn}]}\n- transactions and incomplete_commits sorted by (commit_lsn, tx); duration = number of writes; writes by seq; incomplete = uncommitted/missing-write txs sorted by tx\n- empty records → checkpoint and empty sets\n\nAdditional:\n- same LSN/epoch conflict check covers ALL versions, including those overwritten by higher epoch or after scan gap\n- transaction execution only for continuous LSN prefix after checkpoint; watermark = last LSN actually scanned, including abort and incomplete commit; not last successful commit\n- committed writes keep all writes sorted by seq including duplicate keys, not compressed into key-value table; restore applies later with last-write-wins\n- seq duplicate/missing/out-of-range → incomplete commit, writes not partially committed\n- count=0 with no writes can commit zero-duration transaction\n- records after gap don't affect pending/ended execution state, but still must complete field & version conflict checks\n\nLet me understand the version conflict: \"每lsn取最高epoch\" — each LSN takes highest epoch. \"任意相同lsn/epoch内容冲突ValueError，相同内容去重\". So for a given LSN, among records with the same lsn and epoch, if their content differs → ValueError; if same content → dedupe. Then for the LSN, the record with highest epoch wins (the effective record).\n\n\"同LSN/epoch冲突检查覆盖全部版本，包括被较高epoch覆盖或位于扫描缺口之后者\" — so we must check conflicts across ALL records, regardless of whether they're in the scan prefix.\n\nLet me think about the input records. All covered. int can't be NaN/Inf. float can be nan/inf (from JSON NaN/Infinity). bool is subclass of int but we handle separately (return early, no finite check needed). \n\nNote: Python's json.loads by default uses parse_constant that returns float('nan') for NaN, float('inf') for Infinity, float('-inf') for -Infinity. (as above)\n\ndef _read_int(obj, key, minimum=None):\n    ...\n\ndef _read_str(obj, key):\n    ...\n\ndef transform(payloads, contract):\n    if len(payloads) != len(contract.get('inputs', [])):\n        raise ValueError('payload count mismatch')\n    data = payloads[0]\n    _check_finite(data)\n    if not isinstance(data, dict):\n        raise ValueError('input must be object')\n    target = _read_int(data, 'target', minimum=0)\n    if 'snapshots' not in data:\n        raise ValueError('missing snapshots')\n    snapshots_raw = data['snapshots']\n    if not isinstance(snapshots_raw, list):\n        raise ValueError('snapshots must be list')\n    \n    snapshots = []\n    seen_ids = set()\n    for idx, s in enumerate(snapshots_raw):\n        if not isinstance(s, dict):\n            raise ValueError('snapshot must be object')\n        sid = _read_str(s, 'id')\n        if sid in seen_ids:\n            raise ValueError('duplicate snapshot id')\n        seen_ids.add(sid)\n        kind = _read_str(s, 'kind')\n        if kind not in ('full', 'incremental'):\n            raise ValueError('invalid kind')\n        parent = s.get('parent')  # may be missing? parent required field\n        if 'parent' not in s:\n            raise ValueError('missing parent')\n        parent = s['parent']\n        if parent is not None and not isinstance(parent, str):\n            raise ValueError('parent must be str or null')\n        if isinstance(parent, str) and parent.strip() == '':\n            # empty string parent? treat as unknown? Let's require non-empty if str\n            raise ValueError('parent must be non-empty or null')\n        start = _read_int(s, 'start', minimum=0)\n        end = _read_int(s, 'end')\n        if end <= start:\n            raise ValueError('end must be > start')\n        cost = _read_int(s, 'cost', minimum=0)\n        epoch = _read_int(s, 'epoch', minimum=0)\n        if 'valid' not in s or not isinstance(s['valid'], bool):\n            raise ValueError('valid must be bool')\n        valid = s['valid']\n        if 'state' not in s or not isinstance(s['state'], list):\n            raise ValueError('state must be list')\n        state_raw = s['state']\n        state = []\n        seen_keys = set()\n        for item in state_raw:\n            if not isinstance(item, dict):\n                raise ValueError('state item must be object')\n            key = _read_str(item, 'key')\n            if key in seen_keys:\n                raise ValueError('duplicate state key')\n            seen_keys.add(key)\n            if 'value' not in item:\n                raise ValueError('state item missing value')\n            value = item['value']\n            state.append({'key': key, 'value': value})\n        snapshots.append({\n            'id': sid, 'kind': kind, 'parent': parent, 'start': start,\n            'end': end, 'cost': cost, 'epoch': epoch, 'valid': valid,\n            'state': state\n        })\n    \n    by_id = {s['id']: s for s in snapshots}\n    # parent reference validation (unknown + cycles) for all str parents\n    for s in snapshots:\n        if s['parent'] is not None:\n            if s['parent'] not in by_id:\n                raise ValueError('unknown parent')\n    # cycle detection\n    # color 0 unvisited, 1 in stack, 2 done\n    color = {}\n    def dfs(sid):\n        color[sid] = 1\n        parent = by_id[sid]['parent']\n        if parent is not None:\n            if color.get(parent) == 1:\n                raise ValueError('parent cycle')\n            if color.get(parent) == 0:\n                dfs(parent)\n        color[sid] = 2\n    for sid in by_id:\n        if color.get(sid, 0) == 0:\n            dfs(sid)\n    \n    # full schema check\n    for s in snapshots:\n        if s['kind'] == 'full':\n            if s['parent'] is not None or s['start'] != 0:\n                raise ValueError('full snapshot schema violation')\n    \n    # recoverability\n    memo = {}\n    def recoverable(sid):\n        if sid in memo:\n            return memo[sid]\n        s = by_id[sid]\n        if not s['valid']:\n            memo[sid] = False\n            return False\n        if s['end'] > target:\n            memo[sid] = False\n            return False\n        if s['kind'] == 'full':\n            memo[sid] = True\n            return True\n        p = s['parent']\n        if p is None:\n            memo[sid] = False\n            return False\n        parent = by_id[p]\n        if parent['end'] != s['start'] or parent['epoch'] != s['epoch']:\n            memo[sid] = False\n            return False\n        r = recoverable(p)\n        memo[sid] = r\n        return r\n    \n    # build chains for recoverable snapshots\n    def chain_of(sid):\n        s = by_id[sid]\n        if s['kind'] == 'full':\n            return [s['id']], s['cost']\n        p = s['parent']\n        path, pcost = chain_of(p)\n        return path + [s['id']], pcost + s['cost']\n    \n    candidates = []\n    for sid in by_id:\n        if recoverable(sid):\n            path, cost = chain_of(sid)\n            candidates.append((by_id[sid]['end'], cost, path, sid))\n    \n    if not candidates:\n        return {\n            'name': contract['name'],\n            'result': {\n                'feasible': False,\n                'chain': [],\n                'snapshot_end': 0,\n                'cost': 0,\n                'state': []\n            }\n        }\n    \n    # select best: max end, min cost, lexicographically smallest path\n    best = min(candidates, key=lambda c: (-c[0], c[1], c[2]))\n    end, cost, path, sid = best\n    state = sorted(by_id[sid]['state'], key=lambda x: x['key'])\n    return {\n        'name': contract['name'],\n        'result': {\n            'feasible': True,\n            'chain': path,\n            'snapshot_end': end,\n            'cost': cost,\n            'state': state\n        }\n    }\n```\n\nWait, lexicographic comparison of path lists. Python compares lists lexicographically. \"词典序采用通常字符串/列表/元组顺序，相同前缀时较短列表更小\". Python list comparison does exactly that: compares element-wise, shorter list is smaller if it's a prefix. Good. OK.\n\nNow, let me verify my backup_graph result with a quick mental run or actual script later.\n\nNow let me write journal_restore transform.\n\n```python\nimport json, math\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\ndef _is_int(v): ...\ndef _check_finite(v): ...\ndef _read_int(obj, key, minimum=None): ...\ndef _read_str(obj, key): ...\n\ndef transform(payloads, contract):\n    data = payloads[0]\n    _check_finite(data)\n    if not isinstance(data, dict):\n        raise ValueError('input must be object')\n    checkpoint = _read_int(data, 'checkpoint', minimum=-1)\n    if 'records' not in data:\n        raise ValueError('missing records')\n    records_raw = data['records']\n    if not isinstance(records_raw, list):\n        raise ValueError('records must be list')\n    \n    records = []\n    for idx, r in enumerate(records_raw):\n        if not isinstance(r, dict):\n            raise ValueError('record must be object')\n        lsn = _read_int(r, 'lsn', minimum=0)\n        epoch = _read_int(r, 'epoch', minimum=0)\n        tx = _read_str(r, 'tx')\n        kind = _read_str(r, 'kind')\n        if kind not in ('write', 'commit', 'abort'):\n            raise ValueError('invalid kind')\n        rec = {'lsn': lsn, 'epoch': epoch, 'tx': tx, 'kind': kind}\n        if kind == 'write':\n            key = _read_str(r, 'key')\n            if 'value' not in r:\n                raise ValueError('write missing value')\n            value = r['value']\n            seq = _read_int(r, 'seq', minimum=0)\n            rec.update({'key': key, 'value': value, 'seq': seq})\n        elif kind == 'commit':\n            count = _read_int(r, 'count', minimum=0)\n            rec.update({'count': count})\n        # abort: no extra required\n        records.append(rec)\n    \n    # version conflict: group by (lsn, epoch), dedupe identical, conflict if distinct\n    from collections import defaultdict\n    by_lsn = defaultdict(dict)  # lsn -> {epoch: canonical record}\n    for rec in records:\n        lsn = rec['lsn']\n        epoch = rec['epoch']\n        if epoch in by_lsn[lsn]:\n            existing = by_lsn[lsn][epoch]\n            if existing != rec:\n                raise ValueError('version conflict')\n            # same content -> dedupe (keep existing)\n        else:\n            by_lsn[lsn][epoch] = rec\n    \n    # effective record per lsn = highest epoch\n    effective = {}\n    for lsn, epochs in by_lsn.items():\n        max_epoch = max(epochs)\n        effective[lsn] = epochs[max_epoch]\n    \n    # scan continuous prefix\n    sorted_lsns = sorted(effective.keys())\n    watermark = checkpoint\n    required = checkpoint + 1\n    scan_lsns = []\n    for lsn in sorted_lsns:\n        if lsn < required:\n            continue  # below checkpoint, ignore\n        if lsn == required:\n            scan_lsns.append(lsn)\n            watermark = lsn\n            required += 1\n        else:\n            break  # gap\n    \n    # process scan prefix\n    tx_state = {}  # tx -> {'writes': {seq: {key,value}}, 'dup_seq': bool, 'ended': None|'commit'|'abort', 'commit_lsn':.., 'count':..}\n    def get_state(tx):\n        if tx not in tx_state:\n            tx_state[tx] = {'writes': {}, 'dup_seq': False, 'ended': None, 'commit_lsn': None, 'count': None}\n        return tx_state[tx]\n    \n    transactions = []\n    incomplete_commits = []\n    incomplete_set = set()  # txs that are incomplete (uncommitted or missing-write)\n    \n    for lsn in scan_lsns:\n        rec = effective[lsn]\n        tx = rec['tx']\n        st = get_state(tx)\n        if st['ended'] is not None:\n            raise ValueError('record after commit/abort for tx')\n        if rec['kind'] == 'write':\n            seq = rec['seq']\n            if seq in st['writes']:\n                st['dup_seq'] = True\n            st['writes'][seq] = {'key': rec['key'], 'value': rec['value']}\n        elif rec['kind'] == 'commit':\n            count = rec['count']\n            st['ended'] = 'commit'\n            st['commit_lsn'] = lsn\n            st['count'] = count\n        elif rec['kind'] == 'abort':\n            st['ended'] = 'abort'\n    \n    # finalize\n    for tx in sorted(tx_state.keys()):\n        st = tx_state[tx]\n        if st['ended'] == 'commit':\n            count = st['count']\n            writes = st['writes']\n            seqs = set(writes.keys())\n            if st['dup_seq'] or seqs != set(range(count)):\n                # incomplete commit\n                incomplete_commits.append({'tx': tx, 'commit_lsn': st['commit_lsn']})\n                incomplete_set.add(tx)\n            else:\n                # complete\n                wlist = [writes[i] for i in range(count)]\n                transactions.append({\n                    'tx': tx,\n                    'commit_lsn': st['commit_lsn'],\n                    'writes': wlist,\n                    'duration': count\n                })\n        elif st['ended'] == 'abort':\n            pass  # discarded\n        else:\n            # uncommitted (open)\n            incomplete_set.add(tx)\n    \n    # sort\n    transactions.sort(key=lambda t: (t['commit_lsn'], t['tx']))\n    incomplete_commits.sort(key=lambda t: (t['commit_lsn'], t['tx']))\n    incomplete = sorted(incomplete_set)\n    \n    return {\n        'name': contract['name'],\n        'result': {\n            'watermark': watermark,\n            'transactions': transactions,\n            'incomplete': incomplete,\n            'incomplete_commits': incomplete_commits\n        }\n    }\n```\n\nWait, there's an issue with incomplete_set: for incomplete commits, the tx is added to incomplete_set. Good.\n\nNow, let me write restore_join transform.\n\n```python\nimport json, hashlib\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\ndef _is_int(v): ...\ndef _check_finite(v): ...\ndef _read_int(obj, key, minimum=None): ...\ndef _read_str(obj, key): ...\n\ndef _unwrap(obj):\n    if isinstance(obj, dict) and set(obj.keys()) == {'name', 'result'}:\n        return obj['result']\n    return obj\n\ndef transform(payloads, contract):\n    if len(payloads) != 3:\n        raise ValueError('restore_join needs 3 payloads')\n    for p in payloads:\n        _check_finite(p)\n    \n    win = payloads[0]\n    if not isinstance(win, dict):\n        raise ValueError('input must be object')\n    target = _read_int(win, 'target', minimum=0)\n    rto = _read_int(win, 'rto', minimum=0)\n    expected = _read_str(win, 'expected_sha256')\n    if len(expected) != 64 or any(c not in '0123456789abcdef' for c in expected):\n        raise ValueError('expected_sha256 must be 64 lowercase hex')\n    \n    rc = _unwrap(payloads[1])\n    tr = _unwrap(payloads[2])\n    if not isinstance(rc, dict) or not isinstance(tr, dict):\n        raise ValueError('upstream result must be object')\n    \n    feasible = rc['feasible']  # must be bool\n    if not isinstance(feasible, bool):\n        raise ValueError('feasible must be bool')\n    snapshot_end = _read_int(rc, 'snapshot_end', minimum=0)\n    chain_cost = _read_int(rc, 'cost', minimum=0)\n    if 'state' not in rc or not isinstance(rc['state'], list):\n        raise ValueError('state must be list')\n    base_state = rc['state']\n    # validate base_state items\n    for item in base_state:\n        if not isinstance(item, dict) or 'key' not in item or 'value' not in item:\n            raise ValueError('invalid state item')\n    \n    watermark = _read_int(tr, 'watermark', minimum=-1)\n    if 'transactions' not in tr or not isinstance(tr['transactions'], list):\n        raise ValueError('transactions must be list')\n    transactions = tr['transactions']\n    if 'incomplete_commits' not in tr or not isinstance(tr['incomplete_commits'], list):\n        raise ValueError('incomplete_commits must be list')\n    incomplete_commits = tr['incomplete_commits']\n    \n    def fail(reason):\n        return {\n            'name': contract['name'],\n            'result': {\n                'feasible': False,\n                'reason': reason,\n                'applied': [],\n                'state': [],\n                'sha256': '',\n                'cost': 0\n            }\n        }\n    \n    if not feasible:\n        return fail('snapshot')\n    if snapshot_end > target:\n        return fail('snapshot')\n    if watermark < target:\n        return fail('gap')\n    # transaction check\n    for ic in incomplete_commits:\n        cl = ic['commit_lsn']\n        if snapshot_end < cl <= target:\n            return fail('transaction')\n    # apply\n    applicable = [t for t in transactions if snapshot_end < t['commit_lsn'] <= target]\n    applicable.sort(key=lambda t: (t['commit_lsn'], t['tx']))\n    state_map = {}\n    for item in base_state:\n        state_map[item['key']] = item['value']\n    applied = []\n    cost = chain_cost\n    for t in applicable:\n        for w in t['writes']:\n            state_map[w['key']] = w['value']\n        applied.append(t['tx'])\n        cost += t['duration']\n    state = [{'key': k, 'value': state_map[k]} for k in sorted(state_map)]\n    s = json.dumps(state, ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True, separators=(',', ':'), allow_nan=False)\n    sha256 = hashlib.sha256(s.encode('utf-8')).hexdigest()\n    \n    result = {\n        'feasible': True,\n        'reason': 'ok',\n        'applied': applied,\n        'state': state,\n        'sha256': sha256,\n        'cost': cost\n    }\n    if cost > rto:\n        result['feasible'] = False\n        result['reason'] = 'rto'\n    elif sha256 != expected:\n        result['feasible'] = False\n        result['reason'] = 'digest'\n    return {'name': contract['name'], 'result': result}\n```\n\nWait, there's a subtlety about the transaction records. # 备份恢复认证：接口与业务规格\n2: \n3: 修复缺失实现，依据真实输入生成可重建、确定性的JSON产物。以下业务算法的节点、边、窗口或资源是被实现程序的数据语义，不是Agent执行计划。\n4: \n5: ## 实现范围与保护边界\n6: \n7: 组件目录为`products/backup_graph/`、`products/journal_restore/`、`products/backup_windows/`。每个组件实现model.py、transform.py、render.py；根目录实现run_all.py、final_report.py。可添加必要的私有辅助模块。\n8: 不得修改README.md、任务说明、inputs/、任何contract.json、check_module.py、根check.py或integrate.py。已有受保护稳定接口保留。\n9: \n10: 本题初始工作区提供受保护的materialize.py。使用`python3 materialize.py`生成输入、产品契约、局部检查及缺失实现占位；这是材料准备操作，不生成业务答案。脚本校验嵌入清单SHA-256；重复执行保留已有实现并检查受保护生成文件，冲突必须失败。不得改物化器或生成的输入/契约/检查。\n11: \n12: ## 公共函数接口与输入解释\n13: \n14: - `model.load_payload(path: Path)`：读取UTF-8 JSON，对象或数组顶层合法；非法JSON、顶层null/布尔/数字/字符串均抛ValueError。业务字段由transform检查。\n15: - `transform.transform(payloads: list, contract: dict)`：payloads严格按该契约inputs的顺序传入已读取的对象或数组，返回`{\"name\": contract[\"name\"], \"result\": 算法结果对象}`。输出恰有name/result两层；本说明各operation的输出字段均属于result，不得额外嵌套一次name/result。不得修改payloads或contract。\n16: - 输入对象仅当键集合恰好是`{\"name\",\"result\"}`时视为上游组件封装；先读取其result，再使用对应operation约定的字段。原始本地对象不是文件路径，也不当成records或nodes列表。多输入operation必须消费全部指定输入，不能复制固定上游结果。\n17: - `render.build_component(root: Path, contract_path: Path)`：读取该契约及其全部输入，调用transform并返回完整封装；执行组件render.py时写入contract.output。文件路径相对于工作区root；从任意当前目录启动均应正确，缺文件或子命令失败须失败。\n18: - int字段严格排除bool；有限数字允许int/float但排除bool、NaN、Infinity。非空文本先检查去首尾空白后不为空；是否返回去空白/小写形式由各operation明确规定，不全局改写身份字段。列表与字典按规定类型读取，不把null当空集合，不自动把数字字符串转换成数字。\n19: - 文中没有给出默认值的必要字段缺失、非法类型或明确列出的冲突抛ValueError；合法无解使用operation的无解结果，不等同于输入错误。词典序采用通常字符串/列表/元组顺序，相同前缀时较短列表更小。\n20: - contract中的name/operation/inputs/output是接口字段；只有下列业务规则显式使用的算法参数参与计算，不能看到threshold便对所有operation筛选。参数值读取本组件契约，不从样例推断固定常量。\n21: \n22: 本题强化operation（recovery_chain, restore_join, transaction_replay）还要求整个payloads中的嵌套JSON值有限，即使字段未参与业务计算，内含NaN/Infinity也抛ValueError。\n23: \n24: ## 各组件的完整业务规则\n25: \n26: ### products/backup_graph — 分叉备份链的恢复点与成本优化\n27: \n28: operation：`recovery_chain`。具体输入文件、输入顺序和输出路径读取本目录contract.json；下文只定义文件内容的解释，不另给任务调度关系。\n29: \n30: 输入 {target:int>=0,snapshots:[{id,kind:full/incremental,parent:str或null,start:int>=0,end:int>start,cost:int>=0,epoch:int>=0,valid:bool,state:[{key,value:JSON值}]}]}。snapshot ID与每份state的key唯一，未知parent或任意parent环ValueError。full必须parent=null且start=0；incremental只有parent有效、parent.end==start且epoch相等才可恢复（不满足为不可恢复，不是schema错误）。valid=false的节点及其后代不可恢复；只有end<=target可选。先最大化恢复点end，再最小化整条链cost，再按路径ID列表字典序最小选一条。输出 {feasible,chain:list[id],snapshot_end:int,cost:int,state:按key排序的完整恢复点状态}；无可恢复快照时false/[]/0/0/[]。\n31: \n32: 补充边界：state表示该快照终点的完整恢复点状态，不是增量patch；最终state直接取所选链末快照的state，不把祖先state再合并进来。链成本是所选路径各快照cost之和。需要检查所有parent分量中的环和未知引用，即使快照invalid或end超过target也不能隐藏schema错误。incremental的parent=null是不可恢复，非full的schema失败；full的非null parent或start!=0才是schema错误。无可恢复快照与可行的空state快照有不同feasible值。\n33: \n34: 局部检查命令：`python3 products/backup_graph/check_module.py`。运行检查时，契约引用的输入与本组件输出必须真实存在；缺少条件时先完成实际所需的工作，不能吞掉错误或跳过检查。\n35: \n36: ### products/journal_restore — 连续LSN与原子事务重放\n37: \n38: operation：`transaction_replay`。具体输入文件、输入顺序和输出路径读取本目录contract.json；下文只定义文件内容的解释，不另给任务调度关系。\n39: \n40: 输入 {checkpoint:int>=-1,records:[{lsn:int>=0,epoch:int>=0,tx:str,kind:write/commit/abort,...}]}。write含key:str、value:JSON值、seq:int>=0；commit含count:int>=0；abort无额外必需字段。每lsn取最高epoch，任意相同lsn/epoch内容冲突ValueError，相同内容去重；从checkpoint+1连续扫描，首缺口停止。按LSN积累每tx的writes；commit须seq恰为0..count-1，每key可重复并按seq覆盖，之后才一次输出原子事务；缺写commit标为incomplete，不输出部分写。abort丢弃事务。已扫描前缀内commit/abort后又出现同tx记录ValueError。输出 {watermark:int,transactions:[{tx,commit_lsn,writes:[{key,value}],duration:int}],incomplete:list[tx],incomplete_commits:[{tx,commit_lsn}]}；transactions和incomplete_commits按(commit_lsn,tx)，duration为write条数，writes按seq，incomplete含未结束/缺写事务并按tx排序。空records返回checkpoint及空集合。\n41: \n42: 补充边界：同LSN/epoch冲突检查覆盖全部版本，包括被较高epoch覆盖或位于扫描缺口之后者。事务执行只针对checkpoint后的连续LSN前缀，watermark是实际扫描到的最后LSN，包括abort和不完整commit；不是最后成功commit。已成功commit的writes保留全部按seq排序的写入，包括重复key，不提前压缩成键值表；后续restore应用时才由后写覆盖。seq重复、缺失或越界都属于不完整commit，写入不部分提交。count=0且无写时可以提交零duration事务。缺口后记录不影响pending/ended执行状态，但仍需完成字段与版本冲突校验。\n43: \n44: 局部检查命令：`python3 products/journal_restore/check_module.py`。运行检查时，契约引用的输入与本组件输出必须真实存在；缺少条件时先完成实际所需的工作，不能吞掉错误或跳过检查。\n45: \n46: ### products/backup_windows — 快照与原子日志的恢复一致性核验\n47: \n48: operation：`restore_join`。具体输入文件、输入顺序和输出路径读取本目录contract.json；下文只定义文件内容的解释，不另给任务调度关系。\n49: \n50: 输入顺序为 {target:int>=0,rto:int>=0,expected_sha256:64位小写hex}、recovery_chain结果、transaction_replay结果。要求snapshot可行、snapshot_end<=target、日志watermark>=target，且snapshot_end<commit_lsn<=target范围没有incomplete_commits。取该范围的原子事务按commit顺序应用到snapshot.state；不能应用仅有部分writes或aborted事务。耗时=备份链cost+所应用事务duration之和。输出 {feasible,reason:ok/snapshot/gap/transaction/rto/digest,applied:list[tx],state:[{key,value}],sha256:str,cost:int}，state按key；digest为state规范紧凑JSON的SHA256。snapshot/gap/transaction失败输出空applied/state、sha256为空、cost=0；其余失败保留计算结果。失败优先级为snapshot、gap、transaction、rto、digest，不能因目标摘要恰好一致而忽略缺写的已提交事务。\n51: \n52: 补充边界：base.state为完整起点，事务范围严格是(snapshot_end,target]。按(commit_lsn,tx)应用每个原子事务，内部按其writes给定seq顺序覆盖key，保留未被覆盖的快照键；不能只保留日志键或把snapshot_end这一刻的commit再应用。rto/digest失败时也返回已经计算的applied/state/sha256/cost；snapshot/gap/transaction失败才清空这些字段。无应用事务时仍以快照成本和状态计算摘要并检查rto/digest。\n53: \n54: 局部检查命令：`python3 products/backup_windows/check_module.py`。运行检查时，契约引用的输入与本组件输出必须真实存在；缺少条件时先完成实际所需的工作，不能吞掉错误或跳过检查。\n55: \n56: ## JSON产物、集成与最终报告\n57: \n58: 所有写出的JSON文件采用UTF-8、ensure_ascii=False、sort_keys=True、indent=2、allow_nan=False，末尾加一个真实换行字符。紧凑摘要序列化与文件展示格式不同。\n59: `run_all.py`定位工作区，依据真实文件接口完成全部必要计算，执行已有集成与最终报告。不能用文件名排序代替执行条件，不能依赖旧outputs；各子进程失败传播非零退出码。已有`python3 integrate.py`读取组件产物生成`outputs/core_manifest.json`，其完整结构为`{\"components\": {组件名: 该组件的result}}`。\n60: \n61: `final_report.py`读取并解析完整`outputs/core_manifest.json`对象，写`outputs/final_report.json`，恰有title、component_count、sha256、attention四个字段。title准确为`备份恢复认证`；component_count为core_object[\"components\"]的键数。\n62: sha256的输入对象是完整core_object，包含外层components键；不是仅core_object[\"components\"]、不是原文件字节、不是多个文件摘要拼接。序列化准确为`json.dumps(core_object, ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True, separators=(\",\", \":\"), allow_nan=False).encode(\"utf-8\")`，不加文件末尾换行，然后求SHA-256小写hex。\n63: attention是组件名去重排序列表：对每个result，blocked为真、feasible或compatible显式为false、deny>0、score_total>=20中任一条件成立即列入；不存在的字段不触发，不能把空列表、空对象或数值0等同于显式false。每个组件至多列一次。\n64: \n65: ## 验收与执行边界\n66: \n67: 在工作区运行`python3 check.py`。公开检查会清理旧outputs、完成适用的材料物化、运行run_all并检查模块与输出结构；公开通过不代表全部业务语义正确。外部只读验证器另外检查受保护文件、全部预期产物、规范序列化及接口边界输入。\n68: 给Agent的材料只有本任务TASK.md和workspace。作者参考实现、评分器、审计记录、历史输出和其他任务不得作为工作区或模型上下文输入。\n\n(End of materialize.py`生成输入、产品契约、局部检查及缺失实现占位；这是材料准备操作，不生成业务答案。脚本校验嵌入清单SHA-256；重复执行保留已有实现并检查受保护生成文件，冲突必须失败。不得改物化器或生成的输入/契约/检查。\n\n## 公共函数接口与输入解释\n\n- `model.load_payload(path: Path)`：读取UTF-8 JSON，对象或数组顶层合法；非法JSON、顶层null/布尔/数字/字符串均抛ValueError。业务字段由transform检查。\n- `transform.transform(payloads: list, contract: dict)`：payloads严格按该契约inputs的顺序传入已读取的对象或数组，返回`{\"name\": contract[\"name\"], \"result\": 算法结果对象}`。输出恰有name/result两层；本说明各operation的输出字段均属于result，不得额外嵌套一次name/result。不得修改payloads或contract。\n- 输入对象仅当键集合恰好是`{\"name\",\"result\"}`时视为上游组件封装；先读取其result，再使用对应operation约定的字段。原始本地对象不是文件路径，也不当成records或nodes列表。多输入operation必须消费全部指定输入，不能复制固定上游结果。\n- `render.build_component(root: Path, contract_path: Path)`：读取该契约及其全部输入，调用transform并返回完整封装；执行组件render.py时写入contract.output。文件路径相对于工作区root；从任意当前目录启动均应正确，缺文件或子命令失败须失败。\n- int字段严格排除bool；有限数字允许int/float但排除bool、NaN、Infinity。非空文本先检查去首尾空白后不为空；是否返回去空白/小写形式由各operation明确规定，不全局改写身份字段。列表与字典按规定类型读取，不把null当空集合，不自动把数字字符串转换成数字。\n- 文中没有给出默认值的必要字段缺失、非法类型或明确列出的冲突抛ValueError；合法无解使用operation的无解结果，不等同于输入错误。词典序采用通常字符串/列表/元组顺序，相同前缀时较短列表更小。\n- contract中的name/operation/inputs/output是接口字段；只有下列业务规则显式使用的算法参数参与计算，不能看到threshold便对所有operation筛选。参数值读取本组件契约，不从样例推断固定常量。\n\n本题强化operation（recovery_chain, restore_join, transaction_replay）还要求整个payloads中的嵌套JSON值有限，即使字段未参与业务计算，内含NaN/Infinity也抛ValueError。\n\n## 各组件的完整业务规则\n\n### products/backup_graph — 分叉备份链的恢复点与成本优化\n\noperation：`recovery_chain`。具体输入文件、输入顺序和输出路径读取本目录contract.json；下文只定义文件内容的解释，不另给任务调度关系。\n\n输入 {target:int>=0,snapshots:[{id,kind:full/incremental,parent:str或null,start:int>=0,end:int>start,cost:int>=0,epoch:int>=0,valid:bool,state:[{key,value:JSON值}]}]}。snapshot ID与每份state的key唯一，未知parent或任意parent环ValueError。full必须parent=null且start=0；incremental只有parent有效、parent.end==start且epoch相等才可恢复（不满足为不可恢复，不是schema错误）。valid=false的节点及其后代不可恢复；只有end<=target可选。先最大化恢复点end，再最小化整条链cost，再按路径ID列表字典序最小选一条。输出 {feasible,chain:list[id],snapshot_end:int,cost:int,state:按key排序的完整恢复点状态}；无可恢复快照时false/[]/0/0/[]。\n\n补充边界：state表示该快照终点的完整恢复点状态，不是增量patch；最终state直接取所选链末快照的state，不把祖先state再合并进来。链成本是所选路径各快照cost之和。需要检查所有parent分量中的环和未知引用，即使快照invalid或end超过target也不能隐藏schema错误。incremental的parent=null是不可恢复，非full的schema失败；full的非null parent或start!=0才是schema错误。无可恢复快照与可行的空state快照有不同feasible值。\n\n局部检查命令：`python3 products/backup_graph/check_module.py`。运行检查时，契约引用的输入与本组件输出必须真实存在；缺少条件时先完成实际所需的工作，不能吞掉错误或跳过检查。\n\n### products/journal_restore — 连续LSN与原子事务重放\n\noperation：`transaction_replay`。具体输入文件、输入顺序和输出路径读取本目录contract.json；下文只定义文件内容的解释，不另给任务调度关系。\n\n输入 {checkpoint:int>=-1,records:[{lsn:int>=0,epoch:int>=0,tx:str,kind:write/commit/abort,...}]}。write含key:str、value:JSON值、seq:int>=0；commit含count:int>=0；abort无额外必需字段。每lsn取最高epoch，任意相同lsn/epoch内容冲突ValueError，相同内容去重；从checkpoint+1连续扫描，首缺口停止。按LSN积累每tx的writes；commit须seq恰为0..count-1，每key可重复并按seq覆盖，之后才一次输出原子事务；缺写commit标为incomplete，不输出部分写。abort丢弃事务。已扫描前缀内commit/abort后又出现同tx记录ValueError。输出 {watermark:int,transactions:[{tx,commit_lsn,writes:[{key,value}],duration:int}],incomplete:list[tx],incomplete_commits:[{tx,commit_lsn}]}；transactions和incomplete_commits按(commit_lsn,tx)，duration为write条数，writes按seq，incomplete含未结束/缺写事务并按tx排序。空records返回checkpoint及空集合。\n\n补充边界：同LSN/epoch冲突检查覆盖全部版本，包括被较高epoch覆盖或位于扫描缺口之后者。事务执行只针对checkpoint后的连续LSN前缀，watermark是实际扫描到的最后LSN，包括abort和不完整commit；不是最后成功commit。已成功commit的writes保留全部按seq排序的写入，包括重复key，不提前压缩成键值表；后续restore应用时才由后写覆盖。seq重复、缺失或越界都属于不完整commit，写入不部分提交。count=0且无写时可以提交零duration事务。缺口后记录不影响pending/ended执行状态，但仍需完成字段与版本冲突校验。\n\n局部检查命令：`python3 products/journal_restore/check_module.py`。运行检查时，契约引用的输入与本组件输出必须真实存在；缺少条件时先完成实际所需的工作，不能吞掉错误或跳过检查。\n\n### products/backup_windows — 快照与原子日志的恢复一致性核验\n\noperation：`restore_join`。具体输入文件、输入顺序和输出路径读取本目录contract.json；下文只定义文件内容的解释，不另给任务调度关系。\n\n输入顺序为 {target:int>=0,rto:int>=0,expected_sha256:64位小写hex}、recovery_chain结果、transaction_replay结果。要求snapshot可行、snapshot_end<=target、日志watermark>=target，且snapshot_end<commit_lsn<=target范围没有incomplete_commits。取该范围的原子事务按commit顺序应用到snapshot.state；不能应用仅有部分writes或aborted事务。耗时=备份链cost+所应用事务duration之和。输出 {feasible,reason:ok/snapshot/gap/transaction/rto/digest,applied:list[tx],state:[{key,value}],sha256:str,cost:int}，state按key；digest为state规范紧凑JSON的SHA256。snapshot/gap/transaction失败输出空applied/state、sha256为空、cost=0；其余失败保留计算结果。失败优先级为snapshot、gap、transaction、rto、digest，不能因目标摘要恰好一致而忽略缺写的已提交事务。\n\n补充边界：base.state为完整起点，事务范围严格是(snapshot_end,target]。按(commit_lsn,tx)应用每个原子事务，内部按其writes给定seq顺序覆盖key，保留未被覆盖的快照键；不能只保留日志键或把snapshot_end这一刻的commit再应用。rto/digest失败时也返回已经计算的applied/state/sha256/cost；snapshot/gap/transaction失败才清空这些字段。无应用事务时仍以快照成本和状态计算摘要并检查rto/digest。\n\n局部检查命令：`python3 products/backup_windows/check_module.py`。运行检查时，契约引用的输入与本组件输出必须真实存在；缺少条件时先完成实际所需的工作，不能吞掉错误或跳过检查。\n\n## JSON产物、集成与最终报告\n\n所有写出的JSON文件采用UTF-8、ensure_ascii=False、sort_keys=True、indent=2、allow_nan=False，末尾加一个真实换行字符。紧凑摘要序列化与文件展示格式不同。\n`run_all.py`定位工作区，依据真实文件接口完成全部必要计算，执行已有集成与最终报告。不能用文件名排序代替执行条件，不能依赖旧outputs；各子进程失败传播非零退出码。已有`python3 integrate.py`读取组件产物生成`outputs/core_manifest.json`，其完整结构为`{\"components\": {组件名: 该组件的result}}`。\n\n`final_report.py`读取并解析完整`outputs/core_manifest.json`对象，写`outputs/final_report.json`，恰有title、component_count、sha256、attention四个字段。title准确为`备份恢复认证`；component_count为core_object[\"components\"]的键数。\nsha256的输入对象是完整core_object，包含外层components键；不是仅core_object[\"components\"]、不是原文件字节、不是多个文件摘要拼接。序列化准确为`json.dumps(core_object, ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True, separators=(\",\", \":\"), allow_nan=False).encode(\"utf-8\")`，不加文件末尾换行，然后求SHA-256小写hex。\nattention是组件名去重排序列表：对每个result，blocked为真、feasible或compatible显式为false、deny>0、score_total>=20中任一条件成立即列入；不存在的字段不触发，不能把空列表、空对象或数值0等同于显式false。每个组件至多列一次。\n\n## Version conflict: \"每lsn取最高epoch\" — for each LSN, take the highest epoch. \"任意相同lsn/epoch内容冲突ValueError，相同内容去重\". So group records by (lsn, epoch). For records with same lsn and same epoch, if their content differs → ValueError. If identical → dedupe (keep one). \"同LSN/epoch冲突检查覆盖全部版本，包括被较高epoch覆盖或位于扫描缺口之后者\" — conflict check covers ALL versions, even those overwritten by higher epoch or after scan gap.\n\nSo: for each lsn, there may be multiple epochs. I'll write a helper in each transform.py:\n\n```python\nimport math\n\ndef _check_finite(value):\n    if isinstance(value, float):\n        if not math.isfinite(value):\n            raise ValueError('non-finite number')\n    elif isinstance(value, list):\n        for v in value:\n            _check_finite(v)\n    elif isinstance(value, dict):\n        for v in value.values():\n            _check_finite(v)\n```\n\nWait, but bool is a subclass of int, and int is not float. I'll check finite on each payload at the start.\n\nNow let me write everything.\n\nLet me write transform.py for backup_graph first.\n\nI need to think about the exact validation and error cases, and the recoverability/selection.\n\nLet me write helper functions for validation:\n\n```python\nimport math\n\ndef _is_int(v):\n    return isinstance(v, int) and not isinstance(v, bool)\n\ndef _is_finite_number(v):\n    return isinstance(v, (int, float)) and not isinstance(v, bool) and math.isfinite(v)\n```\n\nFor backup_graph:\n\n```python\ndef transform(payloads, contract):\n    for p in payloads:\n        _check_finite(p)\n    if len(payloads) != 1:\n        raise ValueError('recovery_chain expects one payload')\n    data = _unwrap(payloads[0])\n    if not isinstance(data, dict):\n        raise ValueError('input must be an object')\n    if 'target' not in data or 'snapshots' not in data:\n        raise ValueError('missing target or snapshots')\n    target = data['target']\n    if not _is_int(target) or target < 0:\n        raise ValueError('target must be int>=0')\n    snapshots = data['snapshots']\n    if not isinstance(snapshots, list):\n        raise ValueError('snapshots must be a list')\n    by_id = {}\n    for s in snapshots:\n        if not isinstance(s, dict):\n            raise ValueError('snapshot must be object')\n        # id\n        if 'id' not in s:\n            raise ValueError('snapshot missing id')\n        sid = s['id']\n        if not isinstance(sid, str) or sid.strip() == '':\n            raise ValueError('snapshot id must be non-empty string')\n        if sid in by_id:\n            raise ValueError('duplicate snapshot id: %s' % sid)\n        # kind\n        kind = s.get('kind')\n        if kind not in ('full', 'incremental'):\n            raise ValueError('snapshot kind must be full or incremental')\n        # parent\n        parent = s.get('parent')\n        if parent is not None and not isinstance(parent, str):\n            raise ValueError('parent must be string or null')\n        # start\n        start = s.get('start')\n        if not _is_int(start) or start < 0:\n            raise ValueError('start must be int>=0')\n        end = s.get('end')\n        if not _is_int(end) or end <= start:\n            raise ValueError('end must be int>start')\n        cost = s.get('cost')\n        if not _is_int(cost) or cost < 0:\n            raise ValueError('cost must be int>=0')\n        epoch = s.get('epoch')\n        if not _is_int(epoch) or epoch < 0:\n            raise ValueError('epoch must be int>=0')\n        valid = s.get('valid')\n        if not isinstance(valid, bool):\n            raise ValueError('valid must be bool')\n        state = s.get('state')\n        if not isinstance(state, list):\n            raise ValueError('state must be a list')\n        seen_keys = set()\n        state_entries = []\n        for e in state:\n            if not isinstance(e, dict):\n                raise ValueError('state entry must be object')\n            if 'key' not in e or 'value' not in e:\n                raise ValueError('state entry missing key/value')\n            k = e['key']\n            if not isinstance(k, str) or k.strip() == '':\n                raise ValueError('state key must be non-empty string')\n            if k in seen_keys:\n                raise ValueError('duplicate state key: %s' % k)\n            seen_keys.add(k)\n            state_entries.append({'key': k, 'value': e['value']})\n        # full/incremental schema constraints\n        if kind == 'full':\n            if parent is not None:\n                raise ValueError('full snapshot must have null parent')\n            if start != 0:\n                raise ValueError('full snapshot must start at 0')\n        # incremental: parent can be null (unrecoverable) or str\n        by_id[sid] = {\n            'id': sid, 'kind': kind, 'parent': parent,\n            'start': start, 'end': end, 'cost': cost,\n            'epoch': epoch, 'valid': valid,\n            'state': state_entries,\n        }\n    # check unknown parents and cycles\n    for sid, s in by_id.items():\n        p = s['parent']\n        if p is not None and p not in by_id:\n            raise ValueError('unknown parent: %s' % p)\n    # cycle detection\n    # each node has at most one parent; follow chain\n    for sid in by_id:\n        slow = sid\n        seen = set()\n        cur = sid\n        while True:\n            if cur in seen:\n                raise ValueError('cycle detected involving %s' % cur)\n            seen.add(cur)\n            parent = by_id[cur]['parent']\n            if parent is None:\n                break\n            cur = parent\n    # compute recoverability\n    memo = {}\n    def recoverable(sid):\n        if sid in memo:\n            return memo[sid]\n        s = by_id[sid]\n        if not s['valid']:\n            memo[sid] = False\n            return False\n        if s['kind'] == 'full':\n            memo[sid] = True\n            return True\n        p = s['parent']\n        if p is None:\n            memo[sid] = False\n            return False\n        if not recoverable(p):\n            memo[sid] = False\n            return False\n        par = by_id[p]\n        if par['end'] != s['start'] or par['epoch'] != s['epoch']:\n            memo[sid] = False\n            return False\n        memo[sid] = True\n        return True\n    # selection\n    candidates = []\n    for sid, s in by_id.items():\n        if s['end'] <= target and recoverable(sid):\n            # compute chain and cost\n            chain = []\n            cur = sid\n            while cur is not None:\n                chain.append(cur)\n                cur = by_id[cur]['parent']\n            chain.reverse()\n            cost = sum(by_id[x]['cost'] for x in chain)\n            candidates.append((s['end'], cost, chain, sid))\n    if not candidates:\n        result = {\n            'feasible': False, 'chain': [], 'snapshot_end': 0,\n            'cost': 0, 'state': [],\n        }\n    else:\n        # maximize end, minimize cost, lexicographically smallest chain\n        best = min(candidates, key=lambda c: (-c[0], c[1], c[2]))\n        end, cost, chain, sid = best\n        terminal_state = sorted(by_id[sid]['state'], key=lambda e: e['key'])\n        result = {\n            'feasible': True, 'chain': chain, 'snapshot_end': end,\n            'cost': cost, 'state': terminal_state,\n        }\n    return {'name': contract['name'], 'result': result}\n```\n\nWait, the lexicographic comparison: \"按路径ID列表字典序最小选一条\". The chain is a list of id strings. Lexicographic order of lists: compare element by element; shorter list is smaller if prefix equal. Python's list comparison does exactly this. So `key=lambda c: (-end, cost, chain)` works: for max end, we negate; for min cost and min chain, use positive. But wait, min with key returns the smallest. So key = (-end, cost, chain): the candidate with largest end has smallest -end; then smallest cost; then smallest chain (lexicographic). Yes.\n\nBut careful: `chain` is a list of strings. The tuple comparison `(-end, cost, chain)` — cost is int, chain is list. Comparing tuples: first -end (int), then cost (int), then chain (list of str). Let me write it carefully.\n\n```python\nimport math\n\ndef transform(payloads, contract):\n    for p in payloads:\n        _check_finite(p)\n    if len(payloads) != 1:\n        raise ValueError('transaction_replay expects one payload')\n    data = _unwrap(payloads[0])\n    if not isinstance(data, dict):\n        raise ValueError('input must be an object')\n    checkpoint = data.get('checkpoint')\n    if not _is_int(checkpoint) or checkpoint < -1:\n        raise ValueError('checkpoint must be int>=-1')\n    records = data.get('records')\n    if not isinstance(records, list):\n        raise ValueError('records must be a list')\n\n    # Validate all records + version conflict\n    # group by (lsn, epoch) -> list of record dicts (deduped)\n    from collections import defaultdict\n    groups = {}\n    for r in records:\n        if not isinstance(r, dict):\n            raise ValueError('record must be object')\n        lsn = r.get('lsn')\n        if not _is_int(lsn) or lsn < 0:\n            raise ValueError('lsn must be int>=0')\n        epoch = r.get('epoch')\n        if not _is_int(epoch) or epoch < 0:\n            raise ValueError('epoch must be int>=0')\n        tx = r.get('tx')\n        if not isinstance(tx, str) or tx.strip() == '':\n            raise ValueError('tx must be non-empty string')\n        kind = r.get('kind')\n        if kind not in ('write', 'commit', 'abort'):\n            raise ValueError('kind must be write/commit/abort')\n        if kind == 'write':\n            key = r.get('key')\n            if not isinstance(key, str) or key.strip() == '':\n                raise ValueError('write key must be non-empty string')\n            if 'value' not in r:\n                raise ValueError('write missing value')\n            seq = r.get('seq')\n            if not _is_int(seq) or seq < 0:\n                raise ValueError('write seq must be int>=0')\n        elif kind == 'commit':\n            count = r.get('count')\n            if not _is_int(count) or count < 0:\n                raise ValueError('commit count must be int>=0')\n        # abort: nothing extra\n        # dedupe/conflict by (lsn, epoch)\n        gkey = (lsn, epoch)\n        if gkey not in groups:\n            groups[gkey] = r\n        else:\n            if groups[gkey] != r:\n                raise ValueError('conflicting records at same lsn/epoch')\n            # else identical, dedupe (keep existing)\n    # determine highest epoch per lsn -> active record\n    active = {}\n    for (lsn, epoch), r in groups.items():\n        if lsn not in active or epoch > active[lsn][0]:\n            active[lsn] = (epoch, r)\n    # scan\n    scan_lsns = []\n    cur = checkpoint + 1\n    while cur in active:\n        scan_lsns.append(cur)\n        cur += 1\n    watermark = scan_lsns[-1] if scan_lsns else checkpoint\n    # process\n    pending = {}  # tx -> list of (seq, key, value)\n    ended = set()  # tx that committed or aborted\n    transactions = []\n    incomplete_commits = []\n    incomplete = []  # tx strings (unended + missing-write)\n    missing_write_txs = set()  # txs with incomplete commit\n    for lsn in scan_lsns:\n        epoch, r = active[lsn]\n        tx = r['tx']\n        kind = r['kind']\n        if tx in ended:\n            raise ValueError('transaction already ended: %s' % tx)\n        if kind == 'write':\n            pending.setdefault(tx, []).append((r['seq'], r['key'], r['value']))\n        elif kind == 'commit':\n            count = r['count']\n            writes = pending.get(tx, [])\n            seqs = sorted(w[0] for w in writes)\n            if seqs == list(range(count)):\n                # valid commit\n                sorted_writes = sorted(writes, key=lambda w: w[0])\n                transactions.append({\n                    'tx': tx, 'commit_lsn': lsn,\n                    'writes': [{'key': w[1], 'value': w[2]} for w in sorted_writes],\n                    'duration': len(sorted_writes),\n                })\n            else:\n                # incomplete commit (missing/duplicate/out-of-range writes)\n                incomplete_commits.append({'tx': tx, 'commit_lsn': lsn})\n                missing_write_txs.add(tx)\n            pending.pop(tx, None)\n            ended.add(tx)\n        elif kind == 'abort':\n            pending.pop(tx, None)\n            ended.add(tx)\n    # unended txs\n    for tx in pending:\n        incomplete.append(tx)\n    for tx in missing_write_txs:\n        if tx not in incomplete:\n            incomplete.append(tx)\n    # sort\n    transactions.sort(key=lambda t: (t['commit_lsn'], t['tx']))\n    incomplete_commits.sort(key=lambda t: (t['commit_lsn'], t['tx']))\n    incomplete.sort()\n    result = {\n        'watermark': watermark,\n        'transactions': transactions,\n        'incomplete': incomplete,\n        'incomplete_commits': incomplete_commits,\n    }\n    return {'name': contract['name'], 'result': result}\n```\n\nWait, there's an issue with `seqs == list(range(count))` when count=0 and writes is empty: seqs=[] == list(range(0))=[] → valid. Good.\n\nNow for backup_windows transform.\n\n```python\nimport hashlib, math\n\ndef transform(payloads, contract):\n    for p in payloads:\n        _check_finite(p)\n    if len(payloads) != 3:\n        raise ValueError('restore_join expects three payloads')\n    window = _unwrap(payloads[0])\n    rc = _unwrap(payloads[1])\n    tr = _unwrap(payloads[2])\n    if not isinstance(window, dict):\n        raise ValueError('window input must be object')\n    target = window.get('target')\n    if not _is_int(target) or target < 0:\n        raise ValueError('target must be int>=0')\n    rto = window.get('rto')\n    if not _is_int(rto) or rto < 0:\n        raise ValueError('rto must be int>=0')\n    expected = window.get('expected_sha256')\n    if not isinstance(expected, str):\n        raise ValueError('expected_sha256 must be string')\n    # (optionally validate 64 lowercase hex)\n    if len(expected) != 64 or not all(c in '0123456789abcdef' for c in expected):\n        raise ValueError('expected_sha256 must be 64 lowercase hex')\n\n    # read rc and tr results\n    if not isinstance(rc, dict) or not isinstance(tr, dict):\n        raise ValueError('upstream results must be objects')\n    # snapshot feasibility\n    rc_feasible = rc.get('feasible')\n    if not isinstance(rc_feasible, bool):\n        raise ValueError('recovery_chain feasible must be bool')\n    snapshot_end = rc.get('snapshot_end')\n    if not _is_int(snapshot_end) or snapshot_end < 0:\n        raise ValueError('snapshot_end must be int>=0')\n    snapshot_cost = rc.get('cost')\n    if not _is_int(snapshot_cost) or snapshot_cost < 0:\n        raise ValueError('snapshot cost must be int>=0')\n    snapshot_state = rc.get('state')\n    if not isinstance(snapshot_state, list):\n        raise ValueError('snapshot state must be list')\n\n    watermark = tr.get('watermark')\n    if not _is_int(watermark) or watermark < -1:\n        raise ValueError('watermark must be int>=-1')\n    tr_transactions = tr.get('transactions')\n    if not isinstance(tr_transactions, list):\n        raise ValueError('transactions must be list')\n    tr_incomplete_commits = tr.get('incomplete_commits')\n    if not isinstance(tr_incomplete_commits, list):\n        raise ValueError('incomplete_commits must be list')\n\n    empty = {'feasible': False, 'reason': 'snapshot', 'applied': [], 'state': [], 'sha256': '', 'cost': 0}\n\n    # snapshot check\n    if not rc_feasible:\n        empty['reason'] = 'snapshot'\n        return {'name': contract['name'], 'result': empty}\n    if snapshot_end > target:\n        empty['reason'] = 'snapshot'\n        return {'name': contract['name'], 'result': empty}\n    #
